The title is two lines, "Teams Are Guided To Perform / With Quality & Precision". Knowledge at hand · Answers Are Always At Hand / While Teams Work; Clear standards · Instructions Reinforce / Standards In Every Mission; Risk awareness · Alerts Warn Teams / About Risks; Extra resources · Links Open / Outside Resources; Any language · Missions Translate / Instantly; Guided execution · Work Is Guided / Step By Step; Training in context · Micro-Trainings Play / Right Inside The Work; Genuine proof · Photos & Videos Are Taken / Inside The Mission, / Never Stored Or Reused (it was three sentences on six lines); Photo proof · Photos Document / Proper Standards; Video proof · Videos Report / Real Conditions; Quality first · Every Closed Mission / Can Be Rated For Quality; Personal accountability · Each Person Rates / Their Own Work; Crowdsourced quality · Peers Rate / Each Other's Work; Total visibility · Managers See Every Proof / In One Place; Any device · The Same Proof Is On / Any Mobile Device; Documented evidence · Evidence Is Toured / With A Swipe; Web gallery · The Web Shows It All / In Gallery View; Remote oversight · Conditions Are Toured / From Anywhere; Clear ownership · Each Proof Shows / Who Performed; Exact timing · And The Exact / Date & Time; Assured quality · Quality Is Assured By In-Mission / Guidance & Constant Oversight. The film is 2:18 with them. They are NOT the CEO's narration, which Variations 2 and 3 and the board still say; a text retyped in this variation is kept apart from theirs (`CUTS['improved-quality-v2'].layouts.ie`, its own id `IQ-Cut-IE`: `edits.cuts['IQ-Cut-IE']`; `cut-texts` / `cut-text` change the ones of the variation that plays).
  - **The words slide** (`story(words, {slide: true})`): the blue label comes in alone from the left, the white words are then written, the whole goes on to the right; the opening title is typed where it stands, with no yellow line under it.
  - **A title and its action:** the action starts the moment the last word is written (`after`), and words whose action has ended go at most 0.3 s after it (`tail`, `GONE`: after a clip, a swipe, the last tick); words that only show something outlined keep the time they had.
  - **Scene changes:** none opens on its words in the middle any more. The words slide out, the art there is goes in 0.58 s (`TR`, 30% faster than a move), the new art comes in in 0.58 s as its label slides in (`scene`, `changes`, the layout's own `artAt`).
  - **The mission's words are BESIDE it and follow what they are about** (the CEO's rule of 2026-11-09: no tablet, the words next to it, "moving up and down with what they are about"): the big 9:16 mission and its words are one group in the middle (`MISSION_IE`, `WORDS_IE`), the blue label over the white words as in Increased Efficiency's Variation 7 (`BESIDE`, `BesideBand`); "Add Instructions…" is written in the middle of the empty picture, glides to the right, and the mission comes in at its left; each next sentence comes up into place once the one before has gone up. Each one's middle is put, every frame, at the middle of what it is about as the picture shows it (`followOf` in the plan → `BesideBand` measures it, kept 24 px inside the picture): the instructions, the alert, the link, the translate button, the Yes being ticked (the way from the last Yes to the next as the list goes up, so the words stay at the place every Yes lands on), the training's checkpoint, the proofs' checkpoints. Any cut can ask for it: `followOf(c, t)` -> `{a, b, p, within}`.
  - **The tablets, the ratings, the web report and the closing words keep their words in the band**, and that art is drawn `ART_DY` (27) px lower so it has the same room over it as under it (`cut.artDy`, the `.rc-iq-art` layer; `ART_DY` and `WORDS_TOP` are in lib/remotion/cutCore.mjs now, Increased Efficiency's cut takes them from there). The color behind the band's words is never drawn (nothing of this film goes behind them). The missions on a tablet have Increased Efficiency's deeper shadow at rest (`.rc-iq-ie`).
  - **Not brought over:** Increased Efficiency's film has no cursor and no yellow outline (people act, things pop); this one keeps both, since its actions are presses and what it shows is inside scrolling lists. The ratings' words are in the band, not beside the card (the peers' ratings take the card's right).
  - **Rendering it:** the videos project's composition `IQ-Cut-IE` (src/iqcut/IQCut.jsx with `layout: 'ie'`, `CUT_WORDS_IE`, `CUT_FRAMES_IE`; `npm run render:iq-cut-ie` -> out/improved-quality-cut-ie.mp4).
- **Live Oversight's Variation 2, look `remotion`: its Remotion cut, told as the two films before it are (the user, 2026-11-11: "now do a variation in live oversight using the same way we did both previous videos, make sure to use remotion"; written, NOT played or rendered by Claude):** id `v2`, "Variation 2 · Remotion cut" (`variation-add remotion "…" --video live-oversight-v2`), the one that plays on the All videos page; the board's slides and its videos are untouched. About 1:44.
  - **The files:** lib/remotion/oversightCut.mjs (the plan: words, beats, what each page shows by the clock) and components/remotion/OversightCut.jsx (+ `.css`, `.rc-lo-*`); registered in `CUTS['live-oversight-v2']` (id `LO-Cut`: its retyped words are `edits.cuts['LO-Cut']`), `CUT_COMPONENTS` and the look `remotion`'s `boards`.
  - **The art is ONE big board** (the CEO's rules: a big board has its words at the bottom, 2026-11-09, and goes edge to edge, 2026-10-20): the web window's whole 1600 px page across the picture, from its top to the band of the words (`BOARD`, 1600 x 744), never moved or zoomed. It is the web app's own pages as the section's videos draw them: the Overview (components/OverviewScene.jsx, driven by a frame: view, scroll, the mission's details, the viewer, the Name column's menu, History's filter and grouping), the Data Board (DataReport.jsx), the Usage Report (UsageReport.jsx). A click in the top bar brings the new page in over the old one in 0.3 s (`pagesAt`). The viewer's video is played by the cut's clock (`OverviewScene` takes a `Video` now; here `CutVideo`).
  - **Told as the others:** the words slide (blue label alone, white words written, out to the right; the title typed in place), the action starts as the last word is written and the words go 0.3 s after it (`acted`), what is only shown stays 1.1 s (`shown`, several things 0.45 s apart: `each`).
  - **Its words** (`SAYS`: a benefit as the label, one plain sentence on two lines; they are not the narration's, which the board's slides still say): Live rhythm · Feel The Business Rhythm / In Real Time; Aging missions · Open Missions Show / How Long They Have Waited; Work in progress · Claimed Missions Show / What Is Being Done Now; Completed work · Closed Missions Show / What Was Completed; Clear ownership · Every Mission Shows / Who Performed It; Measured execution · Execution Duration Is / Measured Per Mission (Increased Efficiency's own sentence); Full detail · Any Mission Opens / To Its Details; Photo proof · Photos Are Retrieved / In One Click; Video proof · Videos Play / Right On The Board; Collected data · Every Answer Collected / Is There To See; Live dashboards · Collected Data Feeds / Live Dashboards; Instant correction · Anomalies Are Flagged / For Instant Correction; Stored history · Performance History / Is Always Stored; By mission · Find Any Mission's / Past Runs; By person · See The Runs / Of Each Person; By checkpoint · Compare Answers / Checkpoint By Checkpoint; Gallery view · Proofs Line Up / In Gallery View; Evolution over time · Managers Inspect How An Area / Evolves Over Time; Constant visibility · Monitor Trends, Audit Conditions / & Keep Constant Visibility. The title: Remotely Monitor Operations / From Anywhere. There is no closing title card: the last sentence is said over the gallery.
  - **The outlines** are the board's videos' (the CEO, 2026-10-20): the missions in a state as ROWS, from their number to that state's column, with the column's title (open, claimed, closed); who performed and the duration as columns. `BoardMarks` in the component measures them on the table each frame (`rowsRect`, lib/sections/rows.mjs; a column: its title to the bottom of the list, kept over the band); the single things (the three measurements, the report's statements, the flagged answers, the date range) are every cut's `mark`.
  - **The cursor** is every cut's now (`cursorPlan` in lib/remotion/cutCore.mjs: Improved Quality's cursor moved there, its film unchanged frame for frame): it only clicks here (the mission, its photos, the next one, Close, the video, Close, Reports, Overview, History, the Name column's ⋮, its search where "Storage Room Check" is typed, Apply, Group by, Person, Reports, Group by, Checkpoint, Gallery View) and leaves after each scene's last click; it does not point at the outlines.
  - **One mission all the way:** today's Storage Room Check: closed on the board, opened, its photos and video, its measurements, the same measurements in the Data Board with the two flagged, its seven runs in History, by person, its proofs by checkpoint, the storage room day after day in the gallery.
  - **Rendering it:** the videos project's composition `LO-Cut` (src/locut/LOCut.jsx, src/locut/cutPlan.js; `npm run render:lo-cut` -> out/live-oversight-cut.mp4); its pictures and clip are already in that project's public/.
  - **The Video proof scene's clip (the user, 2026-11-12, with a picture of "Videos Play Right On The Board": the video was a thin strip at the top of the viewer; written, NOT played):** `CutVideo` is absolute (it fills its parent) and the viewer's media column takes its size from its children, so the clip collapsed to the 44 px player bar's neighbour. The cut's `Clip` (OversightCut.jsx) now gives it a box of its own, `.rc-lo-clip` (760 x 428, the size the demo's `<video>` has in the viewer: LiveOversightDemo.css `.iq-mv-media video`).

## Dynamic Auditing's and Precision Planning's Remotion cuts, and two rules for every cut (the user, 2026-11-12: "do next ones dynamic auditing and precision planing", "using remotion and same style as increased effeciency"; written, NOT played or rendered by Claude)

Both are Variation 2, look `remotion`, id `v2` "Remotion cut" (`variation-add remotion --video <board>`), the one that plays on the All videos page; the boards' slides and videos are untouched. Told as Live Oversight's and Increased Efficiency's are (sliding titles, the action starts as the last word is written, words go 0.3 s after it, what is only shown stays 1.1 s, the cursor only clicks and leaves after each scene).
  - **Two rules for every cut's words (the user, 2026-11-12):** *never more than two lines of words on top of each other* (a label of two or three words and a sentence of two short lines; rewrite it if it is too long: the narration's four-line closing words are told in two); and *when the words are retyped, the first letter of each word is a capital* by default (`titleCase` in lib/remotion/cutWords.mjs, applied by `setCutText` to the title, the labels and the sentences; the texts of the UI (`mission:`, `schedule:`, `person:`, `board:` keys) are kept as typed). Live Oversight's, Increased Efficiency's and Improved Quality's own words were not rewritten.
  - **Dynamic Auditing** (`DA-Cut`, ~1:50; lib/remotion/auditingCut.mjs + components/remotion/AuditingCut.jsx `.rc-ad-*`; `CUTS['dynamic-auditing-v2']`): ONE thread, the North Building Audit: the tablet (the flat black frame, as Improved Quality's) with four audits posted one by one, the cursor opens the audit (a smaller 9:16 phone's panel in the middle, `PANEL`, the words in the band under it), three Yes, a hazard is filmed; the cards that follow (the corrective mission pops in over the audit, the cursor opens it and plays the evidence, it folds into its closed card with the time and who did it, the follow-up audit, two micro-trainings), the lower training as text and a quiz, the Knowledge Base from the menu, a link, the web report's gallery. The art is the layers `stateAt(t).layers`: a screen a click or a new sentence went to comes in over the one before in 0.4 s. The section's own panels are driven by the clock: `AuditingStage` exports `AuditPanel`, `CorrectivePanel`, `TrainingPanel`, `flowOf`, `AUDITS` and takes `place`, `grow` and `scrollMix` (MissionPanel's `scrollMix`: the window between two scroll places, in parts.jsx); a card that pops fades in over the ones that stay (FlowPart with `center={false}`, the group moved by `FLOW_DX` / `FLOW_DY`). Limit: the hazard's and the evidence's clips are the demo's plain `<video>` with a poster, so they show their picture, they do not play.
  - **Precision Planning** (`PP-Cut`, ~1:25; lib/remotion/planningCut.mjs + components/remotion/PlanningCut.jsx `.rc-pl-*`; `CUTS['precision-planning-v2']`): ONE big page edge to edge (the CEO's rule), the words in the band: the locations, the cursor opens the location (its teams), the workload by hour, the cursor opens the team (its missions, the list scrolls to it by the clock), a mission at set times, one that recurs, the hourly counts of a mission outlined, the cursor opens the second team, both missions, the hours of each. **No schedule box (the user, 2026-11-12, with a picture of the stage's "Restroom Cleaning / Every 3 hours, starting at 9:00 AM" box: "this UI doesn't exist in real software"):** the cut leaves it out (`PlanningStage` `noBox` when `bare`) and presses nothing for it; the slides' board still has it, untouched. `PlanningStage` takes `bare` {w, h} (the whole 1600 px across, no pan) and folds rows by numbers (`unit`, `ops`, `kitchen`; `Reveal` takes 0 to 1). The list is scrolled by `ListScroll` (PlanningCut.jsx), a child drawn before the outlines so they are measured on the scrolled list. **Looked at in the browser on 2026-11-12 (`/cut-stage`, `window.__cut.show(board, seconds)`): Precision Planning and every Dynamic Auditing scene drew as planned; the cursor's motion and the render were not looked at.**
  - **Rendering them:** the videos project's compositions `DA-Cut` (src/dacut/DACut.jsx, cutPlan.js; `npm run render:da-cut`) and `PP-Cut` (src/ppcut/PPCut.jsx, cutPlan.js; `npm run render:pp-cut`).
  - **Requesting Maintenance** (the user, 2026-11-12: "do next request maintenance"; `RM-Cut`; lib/remotion/maintenanceCut.mjs + components/remotion/MaintenanceCut.jsx `.rc-ma-*`; `CUTS['requesting-maintenance-v2']`; `npm run render:rm-cut`; written, NOT looked at or played): told as Dynamic Auditing's. ONE thread, the maintenance request: the cursor presses the board's plus, the On-Demand list comes in (by the clock), Maintenance Request is picked and posted on the board; the cursor opens the request (the panel expands), the issue is typed, Yes, a video; the alert ticket pops to the request's right; it turns orange, then red and an escalation alert pops (snap, the product's rule); the ticket board (the compact one), the cursor opens the ticket and plays the evidence, it is claimed (the responder's avatar) and closed (its execution time and who did it outlined). Only what the real software has (the lesson of the schedule box). `MaintenanceStage` exports `missionsOf`, `ticketsOf`, `RequestPanel`, `TicketPanel`, `flowOf` and the panels take `place` and a typed text so far. Its data is the section's `DEFAULTS['requesting-maintenance']`. The cut shares Dynamic Auditing's constants (`PANEL`, `TABLET`, `FLOW_DX`, ...).
  - **The motion Increased Efficiency's missions have, in these three (the user, 2026-11-12, with a picture of Requesting Maintenance's cut: "the motion of popping timers, missions popping in and moving is not there it's like motion is only in title ... fix these three"; written, NOT looked at or played):** components/remotion/MotionBoard.jsx (+ `.css`, `.rc-mt-*`). `MotionTablet` draws the product's board at the state it goes to and, while it goes (`p` < 1), the board it comes from unseen: each card is moved from where it was to where it is, one move (0.76 s, Easy Ease 70%), over the others with the lifted shadow; a card only in `to` POPS (0.34 s, 0.86 to 1, the pop curve, faded; `pops` {mission id: 0 to 1}); the timers run (`elapsed`). `PopFlow` draws the cards of a flow: the ones that stay, each new card popping at its own place, its arrow after it, a timer's pill popping once as its color goes (`--rc-pop`). Dynamic Auditing: the four audits pop in one after the other, the cards that follow pop. Requesting Maintenance: the request pops in at the top of Open and the missions under it glide down, the ticket glides to Claimed and to Closed, the alert and the escalation pop, the ticket's pill pops at orange and at red (`pillPop`). Precision Planning: as a location or a team opens its rows come in one after the other and the count in each hour pops (`RowPop`, PlanningCut.jsx). Limits: the tablet's tab counts change at once.
  - **Timers run, new missions come at the bottom, the responder (the user, 2026-11-12: "new missions always comes from the bottom, timers should alway be running", "in Slow Responses Turn Red ... the timer should go up and pop the timer and change color green -> orange -> red", "show an avatar on the left and takes the second ticket (it should be called maintenance ... and it should be the one the cursor clicks)"; written, NOT looked at or played):** the section's missions have a FIXED time (`ageSeconds`, `executionSeconds`) so the board showed fixed timers; `running` (MotionBoard.jsx) turns them into counts on the board's `elapsed` (a mission posted in the film starts at 00:00:00 there, a claimed one's execution timer where it is claimed, a closed one stops: `runs` of the plan), `tickChip` / `PopFlow` `since` do it for the cards of a flow (a gray card stays frozen). A new request or ticket comes at the BOTTOM of Open (`bottom` of `missionsOf` / `ticketsOf`; the ticket is the SECOND of Open and it is the one the cursor opens). "Slow Responses Turn Red": the alert ticket's timer goes up at 900 s a second from 00:00:20 (`AGING_RATE`), green, orange at 00:30:00, red at 01:00:00, the pill pops at each color (`pillPop`), then the escalation alert pops. "Who responds": the responder's avatar (Carla) pops at the left of the tablet, goes to the ticket, goes with it to Claimed and settles where its performer is (`Responder`, MaintenanceCut.jsx; `ticketsOf` `bare`: claimed with no avatar yet); the cursor-opened ticket of "The Video Goes With The Ticket" is the same card. Dynamic Auditing's audits and cards run too.

`grabs` in the spec, 0.9 s on After Effects' curve; the same slide's clock moves both, and `near` in `Team` counts the screen's 1.03, so the avatar does not drift on the card), goes to the name's place on it, then goes straight back to the right end of the row, with no second trip to the mission, as it moves to Closed, on top of the column (the user, 2026-10-30: "then it should go to dock bottom right without going to the right of the mission"); then the next one on the far left. **The name comes in by the clock** (the same day: "just fade in the name, bold a bit then unbold, the weight of the text should be smooth bolding, not harsh transitioning"; `names` of a video's profile, `NAME_IN` in the spec, `namesAt` in `components/storyboardPlayerDom.js`, only in "What it shows" and its twins): a slide with a name the slide before does not have fades it in from 0.2 s after the slide starts, over 0.3 s, as the avatar leaves; then a text stroke (`stroke`, so the letters do not move and the weight goes smoothly, which a font's own weights do not) grows over 0.3 s and goes over 0.4 s. **The timers count an hour in this scene** (the same day: "the timers should be faster in this scene mimiquing an hour passed and a lot of work has been done"; `HOUR_PASSES` in the spec: 200 seconds a second over its 25 or so seconds, about 1 h 20 min, after "make it faster a bit more"; `tick` of the profile). In "To Claim A Mission", with the personal code, a claim is the same grab, from where the avatar stands by its code panel; a close there is as it was, by its code panel. Ben takes Inventory Check, Maya Restock Front Desk, Adam Clean Break Room; Prepare Workspace stays in Open and the row ends as it began. The others close up half a place when one leaves and are pushed the other half when it comes back (`ROW.closeUp`: 1 has them take the whole place at once, and nothing left to push). No personal code here: it is 12 slides as it is, about 7 s (`QUEUE`, see "An avatar of the queue stops only on the mission" just below); the code is in the claim and the close scenes. The three names in Closed are outlined together at the end, as who took each was before.

  **An avatar of the queue stops only on the mission** (the user, 2026-11-03: "the motion of the action is weird"; then, having watched a version with no stop at all, not even on the mission, "no I don't like it, revert back to what we had just make it smoother"; "faster but when moving, not how much the avatar settles"; then, on the picture of an avatar at the name's place on a claimed mission: "the avatars shouldn't stop unless it's in the mission like actually doing the task, otherwise move"; written, NOT tested yet). From the row to the mission, with it to Claimed and to the name's place is one move with no stop; the avatar stops there `ON_MISSION` seconds (0.4), doing the task; then it goes back to the row as the mission moves to Closed, and the next person leaves as it lands.

  A slide is a move and a hold, so anything that goes through several slides stops at each one, and with no hold the next slide would be drawn in the middle of the motion, a stutter at every join. So the avatars are not moved by the slides. `faces` of the video's profile (`QUEUE_FACES` in `lib/sections/increased-efficiency.mjs`, `facePlan` in `components/storyboardPlayerDom.js`) makes each change of an avatar between two slides a leg of its own, in seconds of the video and on a curve of its own; `legsAt` (`lib/storyboardPlayer.mjs`) adds the legs up; `facesAt` draws the result in every picture of those slides, moves and holds alike; `prepare` leaves the avatars out of the slides' animations. So an avatar stops where its legs say and not where a slide does, once they are over it is exactly where the slide has it, and the next slide is got ready in a hold as usual (`prepareAhead`, which no longer makes the animations of a slide with no move).

  In seconds from the moment the avatar leaves the row, at the numbers `paced` multiplies (`QUEUE_PACE`, 1.12: "make it a tiny bit slower"; the one number for how fast it all moves, the stop on the mission is not in it), about 2.35 s a person: it reaches the mission at 0.55, the mission starts to go at 0.39, just before, and lands at 0.99, the avatar on its edge (the slide's `influence` and `out` are the leg's `curve`: keep them the same); the avatar slides to the name's place from 0.75 to 1.20, as the mission lands, and stops there 0.4 s; it is back in the row 0.6 later with the mission in Closed, and the next person has left just before. The curves are light, with no slow start; only the two that land have the video's own end (`LAND`: 70%, the second influence of `easyEase`, `out` of a `slides` entry). `QUEUE` has what each slide lasts: `goes to the mission` and `: claimed` have no move, they only change under the avatar; `natural` and `rest` at 0 let a slide be as short as its `hold`. After a move a slide needs about 0.08 s for the next one to be got ready. The name is a little quicker, to fit in its slide (`NAME_IN`). The numbers were picked by working out the avatar's speed: about 1150 px/s on the way to the mission and about 500 to 600 after it, tablet px, with short dips at the two turns. Nothing is recaptured for this.

  **The claim and the close move the same way** (the user, 2026-11-03: "fix the movement in "Each Person Uses A Unique Code To Claim And To Close Missions"", make it match the queue; written, NOT tested yet). `CODE_QUEUE` and `CODE_FACES` are `QUEUE` and `QUEUE_FACES` with one step changed, in "One shared tablet" and its twins: the same steps at the same pace, the same stop on the name's place, the next person leaving as the one before lands. The one more stop is at the mission's edge, to type: the avatar lands there (`LAND`) as the code panel comes in, so `goes to the mission` has a move here (the panel fades in by the slide), and it stays for the dots, whose beat is their own (`CODE_PACE`). From there the carry and the way to the name go on as in the queue; a close goes from the name's place to the mission's edge, types, and goes back to the row as the mission moves to Closed. `types the code` is in `CODE_FACES` with nothing to move, so the avatars are drawn on it too. An avatar under a code panel still fades by the slide (`covered` in the stage): `notPlaced` takes only the place and the size out of the slide's animations.

  **The team at the left of the tablet, and back with a fade (the user, 2026-11-03, with a green rectangle drawn on a picture of the video: "put these avatars to the left side of tablet, and when closing a mission, just fade in in left side don't go back with an animation"; captured, NOT played by Claude):** the three avatars are a column over the tablet's left edge, no longer a row at its bottom right: 100 px in the video, 8 px apart, 6 px from the picture's left edge (over the frame, which starts at x 44, and the screen's edge; the cards of Open start at x 92), the column ending at y 821, just inside the screen's bottom (`TEAM_AT` in `components/sections/EfficiencyStage.jsx`, `y` is the top of place 0, places 1 to 3 are used; `left` is the same for all, `top` goes by `placeOf`). Everywhere the team is: the team board, the shared tablet, the claim and the close, What it shows. The row's order is the column's from the top: the first person (Adam) is at the bottom and pops first, the one who goes first in the queue (Ben) is at the top.
  - **A close is a fade, not a trip back:** as the mission goes to Closed, the avatar fades out where it is (on the card, at the name's place, in the queue; by the code panel in the claim and the close) and fades in at the bottom of the column. `BACK` in the spec, the `': closed'` entry of `QUEUE_FACES` and `CODE_FACES`: `fade` [out, in] (0.22 and 0.45 s) in place of `seconds`; in the queue it starts 0.11 s before the mission moves (`at`), so the avatar is all but gone before the card leaves it, at the end of the stop on the mission; in the claim and the close at the start of the slide, with the code panel. The player (`facePlan`): a leg with `fade` gets a `cut`, the second at which its whole change comes at once (`legsAt`), and `legsFade` (both in `lib/storyboardPlayer.mjs`) gives how much is seen around it; `facesAt` draws the opacity only while it fades and gives the slide's own back after (`faceSeen`), and `prepare` gives it back before a move is got ready (`facesOwn`), or a move prepared in the middle of a fade would fade it again by the slide.
  - **The others close up a whole place** when one leaves (`ROW.closeUp` 1; `placeOf` had `1 - closeUp`, which gave the same half place at 0.5 and none at 1), so the bottom place is free for the one who comes back and nothing is pushed. The queue still ends as it began.
  - The ids of the slides did not change (no step added), so the board's edits stay on them. The frames before the capture are not in git (`public/storyboard/increased-efficiency-v3.json` is untracked).
- **Two options of a video's profile for this (`lib/storyboardVideos.mjs` PROFILES / a chunk's `profile`; `components/storyboardPlayerDom.js`):** `slides: {<words of a slide's id or label>: {move, hold, influence}}` gives a slide its own time (seconds to
  come in, seconds in all: the frame's label is "End of the chapter: <the end step's name>", its id `<chapter>/<step>`, so `'urgency/'` is every slide of a chapter; `influence`: the move goes by After Effects' Easy Ease at that influence on both sides, `easyEase` in `lib/storyboardPlayer.mjs`), and `dots: {lead, each, fade, tail}`: a slide whose code (`data-sec-dots="n"` on the row of boxes) has more dots than the slide before draws the
  new `.sec-dot`s by the clock, one every `each` seconds, each fading and growing over `fade` (`dotsAt`, like `staggerAt`); the move into that slide is 0.03 s, so the engine's own morph does not fade them in together, and the slide stays until the
  last one is in. Increased Efficiency's "One shared tablet" uses both (`CODE_PACE`): the avatar flies at the video's one speed, the dots take 0.05 s each.
- **The missions changing places, slowly (the user, 2026-10-30: "the missions just move super fast, it should be slow, but on a curve animation ... like in after effects where u have 70% left and right, so it's slow faster a bit then slow"; written, NOT tested yet):**
  a slide with no time of its own has `natural * 0.55`, 0.17 s at the pace of `FAST`. Every slide of "Timer Colors" (`MISSIONS_PACE`: the third mission coming in, the three rises) takes 1.3 s on Easy Ease 70%, then 0.45 s still (first 1.5 s; the user, 2026-10-30: "make it faster like a tiny bit"). A card on its own glides whole (`data-sec-chip` in `MOVE_KEYS`), so its schedule goes with it, and one that rises passes over the one going down (`glideFrom`); `flip` does not pair the inside of two cards that only changed places, or a schedule stayed behind fading.
- **The schedules leave at the points (the user, 2026-10-30: "when we reach reward points slowely fade out the schedule and center the missions, start centered then show schedule push them to the left, then center again when fade out"; the pushing and the centering were for the cards on black, and went with them on 2026-11-02: the cards keep their place in the tablet's column):**
  at "Reward Points" (`points/words` in `MOVES`) the schedules fade out, at the video's one speed. There are no schedules from there on (`sched: 0` in `points`, `urgency`, `priority`). The schedules stay 0.7 s once they are in (`schedule/end`). `FlowPart` does not count a `.sec-after` that is hidden.
- **The tablet stays when the video zooms in to the missions (the user, 2026-11-02, over the CEO's "you don't have a tablet in that" of 2026-10-28: "when we zoom in here to missions, u should keep tablet, and just zoom in to tablet and cut right side"; the sentence ended there, read as "for the words"; captured 2026-11-02, NOT played yet):**
  from "Schedules, points and urgency" on, no cards on black: the words are written over the board the video before ends on, the tablet zooms in on its Open column, the gray side cuts it, and it stays there until it zooms out to the board "One shared tablet" opens on. The cards are the tablet's own, so the rises and the pull happen on the board.
  - **The zoom** (`zoom` in the state, `ZOOM` in `components/sections/EfficiencyStage.jsx`, `zoom` of `TabletPart`): the tablet's `transform`, not the player's. 1.45 times the video's fitted tablet: its frame's top left corner 16 px from the picture's edges, Open and Claimed in the picture, a little of Closed under the gray side, the third card ending 40 px over the bottom. `left` and `top` stay as they are, so the video glides it by where it is (`morph`, `glideSpan`), a straight camera move. It goes past the picture, so `tabletFit` leaves it alone and the gray side goes to `PANEL_EDGE` (`narrowPanel`).
  - **The cut** (`data-sec-cut` on the tablet, `cut` in `extract`, `layersOf` in `lib/sections/captureCore.mjs`): on a zoomed slide the Panel layer comes after the art, so it is over it (`fitArt` then leaves it alone; a layer's place alone does not stack it: `applyEdits` stacks by order only on a slide with an `order` edit, and `.adx-tablet` has a z-index and the panel none, so `[data-sec-cut] ~ [data-sb-root="panel"]` in `components/sections/sections.css` gives the panel the tablet's z-index, and being after it the panel is over it; before that rule the tablet went over the gray side whenever a slide held and only a move raised the panel: the user, 2026-11-02, "sometimes it goes above the right side gray area"). The fitted tablet is seen to end at x 1172, exactly where the gray side of the zoom starts, so in the move the panel over the software stands still there and cuts it from the first moment; the one under the software glides to its place, for what is seen over and under the tablet (`prepare`, the panel branch; a zoom out has a copy of the panel over the new tablet).
  - **The words in those moves:** the old slide is under the new one, so under a panel that is over the software in it, and its words would go at once. They go on in a copy over that panel (`overPanel` in `prepare`), whether they stay or change.
  - **First slide** (`fromBefore` of a chapter, `schedule/before`, label "As the scene before ends"): the picture the chapter before ends on. A `/start` slide is dropped from a video's opening and from before its words, so this is what the video opens on; the rule "the right side is never empty" brings the words onto it, so the pen writes on the whole board and the zoom is the move of `schedule/words` (`/words` in `MOVES`, the video's one speed). The video `joins` the one before (no opening, the timers go on, its pen waits for the old words).
  - **Two, one, three missions, the rest dimmed (the user, 2026-11-02: "do not remove other missions, just dim everything when we want to show one mission etc"; the first go hid the others)** (`cards`, `one`, `litOf`): all four stay on the board, nothing is laid out again and the column says "Open - 4". Everything but the cards shown is under a shade: `.sec-shade`, `DIM` 0.5, drawn by `shade` of `TabletPart` through `demoBoard` of `InteractiveMissionBoard`. It has to be INSIDE `.mi-tablet-board`: that is a stacking context of its own (`container-type`), so the board's `.mi-demo-shade`, outside it, would dim the cards too. The cards shown have z-index 4 over the shade's 3 (`Over`), a card that rises 5 for its move (`glideFrom`), the schedules are over all of it (`.sec-over`). The shade's opacity morphs with the zoom. The zoom out has `lit`: the cards shown stay over the shade as it fades, else they would dim for a moment. The schedules are in `Over`, placed by measuring beside their card (`data-for`).
  - **Outlines:** `Over` puts the 1.03 the boards give the screen on it while zoomed in, so what the capture measures is what is seen: 3% of a zoomed tablet is a dozen px. `chip-points` is a target of `TabletPart` now (`card-<id>-points`).
  - `glideFrom`'s pull and `zIndex` take a board's card too (`data-mission-id`), not only a card on its own: a board's card that rises passes over the others in every video.
  - **The two closed missions the same way (the user, 2026-11-02: "zoom in on tablet same way on missions in 0:55 to 1 minute for those two completed missions"; captured, NOT played yet):** `execution` and `performers` are `zoomed({zoom: 2})`: `ZOOM[2]` has the same size and height as `ZOOM[1]`, on the Closed column. That is the tablet's last column, so the frame's right edge stays where the fitted tablet has it, at the gray side (x 1172): the tablet grows from that edge and nothing runs under the panel, which is still stacked after the art (`data-sec-cut`), so `fitArt` leaves it alone. The Open column is a sliver at the left of the picture, dimmed. `litOf` gives `sig` and `ord`; `lit` is 2 on the zoom out. The chapter is `fromBefore` and the video `joins` the one before, as "Schedules, points and urgency" does. `view: 'closed'`, `view: 'landing'` and the stage's `Cards` are gone: no scene has cards on black any more.
  - `FlowPart`'s `middle` is gone (521 for all).
- **One speed for the whole video (the user, 2026-10-30: "show the missions when saying work in work missions on the right, so show them not as fast, show them with a good animation like the animations speed of the scene timer color create a sense of urgency, please use that animation speed as a reference for all animations in this video"; written, NOT tested yet):**
  `STEADY` (0.76 s on Easy Ease 70%, then 0.26 s still) in `lib/sections/increased-efficiency.mjs` is the one place to change it, `FASTER` the one number for the pace of the whole video. `MOVES` is the video's `slides` table: every step a scene goes on with (`End of the chapter`, the slides' label), every slide a scene's words come on (`/words`), the yellow outlines (a `slides` entry's `fade`: the seconds they take to come in). It replaces the separate paces of the landing, the claim and the queue (the queue has its own again since 2026-11-03: `QUEUE`); the detailed twins get it too (`slides` is not in `PACE`). `HOUR_PASSES` still counts over an hour across the scene: `FASTER` shortens it, so it raises the timers' rate too.
  **The missions of the phones came in with the words** (a `slides` entry's `pen`, seconds after the slide starts): the slide before ("individuals/words") is played with no words and goes on as soon as it is in, so the phones and the gray side come in first, and the pen touches 0.05 s into the first missions' move (its bubble is there from the start of the move: `there`, not `pen-in`, which waits for old words to go). Not in a detailed video: its steps are written on those slides. **No video uses `pen` now:** the user's comment of 2026-10-30 put the words first (see "The comments on Increased Efficiency's videos"); the option is still there.
- **Every move, scale and fade at that one speed, faster (the user, 2026-10-30: "make the animations of movements/scale/opacity on all animations same speed, and make all of them 30% faster", then "make it faster more, especially showing avatars should one after one very fast"; written, NOT tested yet):**
  the times are divided by `FASTER`, 1.7, 30% faster twice (first 1.3), so 1.3 s is now 0.76 s (`quick` in the spec: the numbers in the spec are the times as they were). Holds and stills go down with it. Before this only the slides of `MOVES` had the speed: seven scenes' words came on 0.17 to 0.5 s moves, the title and the software a part opens on took 0.8 s, a name 0.5 s, two parts crossed in 0.45 s. Now:
  - **Every slide with words** (`/words` in `MOVES`), also where only the words change (`priority`, `claim`, `close`, `device`, `performers`, `initiative`, `conclusion`): the old words fade at this speed, the pen touches as they go (0.43 s). `beat: false` in a `slides` entry takes a scene's beat away: `device` and `conclusion` are "plain" scenes, whose pen waits for a highlight they do not have after the move, which would leave the right side empty for a while. `intro/words` keeps its own hold, the title's 0.76 s in plus 1 s to read.
  - **The title and the software a part opens on** (`profile.titleIn`, seconds; else `TITLE.in`), and what comes in with a slide on After Effects' curve goes by that curve too (`onCurve` in `fadesAt`: a title, a part's software, an outline).
  - **A name on a card** (`NAME_IN`): the fade is `STEADY.move`, and `swell` (seconds after it starts, else once it has faded in) has it swell as it comes, so the bold has its 0.29 s up and down and the slide is no longer than the others.
  - **The cross between two parts on the All videos page** (`cross` of the spec, `crossOf` in `lib/storyboardAllVideos.mjs`; every other board keeps 0.45 s and CSS `ease`). `profile.firstPen`: the pen of a part's first slide waits this long, 70% into the cross, by when the old words are all but gone (instead of `JOIN_PEN`, 0.35 s, and it applies to a part that opens with `intro` too).
  - **The avatars of the team coming in** (`TEAM_POPS`, a chunk's `profile.pops`: {label, selector, lead, each, fade, tail}): a run of slides that each add one thing is played as ONE slide, its last, with no move, and the first things of `selector`, one for each slide of the run, pop by the clock, `each` apart (0.09 s), each in `fade` (0.22 s), growing from 0.4 and settling (`SPRING`). Four avatars take 0.6 s, not four slides. By the clock and not as very short slides: the next slide is got ready in a slide's hold, so slides of a few pictures would stutter. It is in `fadesAt` and not with the pens, so the move after it goes from all of them in. It works for both runs (`device`, three; `performers`, three): the board's slides are not touched.
    **The missions of the team board pop with the avatars (the user, 2026-11-02: "show missions and avatars same time same speed as we have avatars right now"; written, NOT tested yet):** `with` {label, selector, tally, tail} of `pops`: the slides of that label right after the run ("mission 1…4") are in the run, only the last is played, and the k-th mission pops with the k-th avatar, at the same `each` and `fade`, in the order of the column (top first). `tally` writes the count ("Open - 0…4") as they come in, since the played slide already says 4. `with.tail`: the 0.26 s still of the video's one speed, this being the end of the video. The cursor of the "cursor" variants goes with those slides: it would otherwise appear on the last mission. Nothing to recapture: the board keeps its 11 slides, only the video changes.
  - **Not at this speed:** the dots of a code keep their own quick beat ("in a smooth fast animation"), as much faster: 0.05 s each, and the avatars above. The pen's 30 letters a second and the timers are not moves. A fade that is a share of its move stays one (`swap-out`, `swap-in`: half of it, `quick-out`: a quarter, `LAND`), so a scene's old words go in the first half of the move.

## The comments on Increased Efficiency's videos (the user's pins of 2026-10-30, worked on 2026-11-02: "based on the comments in the /storyboard fix the videos and add done to the finished comments")

Written and captured (2026-11-02, the user's OK; the words and places below carried over), **not played by Claude** (no build, no test; the slides' colors, missions, avatars and order were read from the captured file). Three comments are done. The two of "Schedules, points and urgency" were marked done too, and the user took that off on their page the same day (two saves that changed nothing else), so they stay open until the user says what is wrong. The comments are read with `node scripts/storyboard.mjs vcomments`. What a comment is about is what the video shows at its second, so the seconds below are worked out from the timeline: a comment's words alone can be read two ways.
- **"finish sentence and then start the action / the action "4 missions""** (Missions assigned to individuals and teams, 4.33 s: the pen was finishing and the first missions were in): the missions of the phones no longer come with the first word (`pen` is off this video). The words are written, then **four missions on each phone** (the user, 2026-11-02: "in first scene 4 missions it means 4 missions per phone"; first read as four in all), 12 in all: four rounds, each phone one more, as the two rounds of before (`phones` is how many each phone has), so about 4 s at the video's one speed (12 one at a time would be 12 s). Each new one is under the others with the shortest timer: the longest is on top (`pillTime`). Four fit: a card is about 78 px tall in the video, the last ends near y 631, the screen near y 780. The chapter's `point` goes with them, the phone at the right.
- **"Show Text, then avatars and then missions in sequence"** (8.06 s: the words written, the board empty): on "Or To Teams…" the words, then the team pops at the bottom right of the tablet (`team`, `TEAM_POPS`: the same row and beat as "One shared tablet"), then the four missions, one after the other. **Since 2026-11-02 the missions pop WITH the avatars, at their speed** (see "The avatars of the team coming in").
- **"Show only One mission before going to three. pop the green and do it fast green,orange red in one second"** (Schedules, points and urgency, 7.39 s: just before the third card came): "Timer Colors…" opens on Order Supplies lit and the rest of the board dimmed (`one`, `ALONE`; see "The tablet stays when the video zooms in to the missions"). **Keep the DOM the same in every state.** The words are written; then one slide with no move (`COLOR_RUN`, a `profile.pulse`: `label`, `selector`, `colors`, `each`, `up`, `down`, `grow`, `influence`, `tail`) draws the pill by the clock in `fadesAt`. **One pop (the user, 2026-11-02: "pop once, go over colors then remove the pop, not pop pop pop, it should be a mature good looking animation"; each color had a pop of its own, 1.3 times its size):** it grows once to 1.18 times its size in 0.29 s, goes green, orange, red, a third of a second each, and settles in 0.29 s, on After Effects' curve; a color changes at once, as a timer's color always does. The slide's own picture is the last color (`pulse` in the stage, also what keeps the slide: one that looks like the slide before is dropped). Then the two others come in round it and its pill is green again.
- **"Pull to the right before going up / orange, green, green / orage, orange, green / red, orange, orange"** (14.41 s, on the bottom card, which was still green): the third state is red, orange, orange (`COLOR.inv`; so the tablet after it is red, orange, orange, green, the cards landing as they are; a fresh board, `more`, stays all green). A mission that goes up goes out to the right by 30% of its width, up beside the others and back in (`pull` of a `slides` entry, `RISE` in the spec, `pullKeys` in `components/storyboardPlayerDom.js`), the card it passes going down over the same time. The legs (`PULL`: out 0 to 42% of the time, up 22 to 82%, back 60 to 100%) are each on the slide's Easy Ease in the TIME of the move: a keyframe's offset is in the move's eased clock, so a path sampled evenly in it would run all its legs in the fast middle. The way is longer than a glide, so these three slides take 1.03 s and not 0.76 s: the same speed, not the same time.
- **"show photo of three people AI generated, and then show the text after this one that says there is no need to provide each person ..."** (One shared tablet, 2.58 s: right after the avatars): the chapter `device` is now right after `shared`, and its art is the picture (`view: 'photo'`, `Photo` in the stage, layer `Board` like the phones, so the video swaps it with the tablet as it does the phones), in the tablet's space as the CEO asked (since 2026-11-02 exactly the tablet's frame: see "From the picture into our tablet"). It is `scripts/image-refs/kitchen-team-board.webp`, the AI picture the user already had (three people looking at the Team Board on the wall: the CEO's "picture of the three people looking at the wall"), made `public/storyboard-art/three-people.jpg` by the job `three-people` (no API call). A "Scene in" slide with no words is not played (`directorOf`), so `wait` of a `slides` entry had the pen touch 0.26 s after the picture is in (no video uses `wait` now: see the next point; the option is still there). Then the picture zooms in to its tablet, ours coming in on it, and the claim and the close follow; the video ends on the close.
- **The picture has "A Whole Team Can Share…", the avatars wait for "There Is No Need To Provide…" (the user, 2026-11-03, on the All videos page: ""A Whole Team Can Share One Single Device" should show image of the three persons, then when u zoom in the tablet and before showing avatars say "There Is No Need To Provide Each Person With Their Own Device" before "Each Person Uses A Unique Code To Claim And To Close Missions""; captured, NOT played by Claude):** "One shared tablet" now goes: the picture, its words, the zoom into our tablet (`shared/end`), "There Is No Need To Provide…" on that tablet with no team on it (`device/words`), the three avatars pop (`device/end` to `end-3`), "Each Person Uses A Unique Code…". It was 45, 41, 1127 x 814, corners of 18 px.

The picture says where the tablet on its wall is: `data-sec-land` on `.sec-photo`, its four corners in the box's px (`WALL_TABLET`, in the picture's px, from lines fitted on its edges to under a pixel). That tablet is seen from the side, about 1.13 wide to 1 tall, ours 1.39, so a plain scale could not keep the same boundaries: at each moment the corners are on their way to the box's (`at`), the picture's `img` goes by the matrix that puts its tablet there and our tablet by the matrix that puts its frame there (`quadMatrix` in `lib/storyboardPlayer.mjs`), 24 samples on the move's clock. Sampled because what the browser makes between two far matrices is not the same for the two: this way one stays on the other, to the pixel. Our tablet's matrix goes at the end of its own `transform`, inside the `scale` and `translate` the video has fitted it with, in px about its origin (`inner`), so it ends exactly as it rests. The old slide stays under the new one, so the picture needs no slide of its own. Nothing of the picture goes behind the camera (w stays 0.98 to 1.7).

First it was a zoom to the full tablet of the picture and then a fade: the picture's own was seen big and soft, and the fade came after a long wait.

The later `LANDING.fade` starts, the less it shows and the more of the picture's own tablet is seen. Keeping the head in front would take a cut-out of it on top.

The layer is named `Isolated missions`, so the video LIFTS it out of the board of `schedule/before` (`liftSpan`, which puts the layer by its FIRST card): the second card is where the board has its own only because it is as far under the first, 129 + 18 of the board's 1080 units (`PAIR.pitch`; `.chip` and `.mi-column-cards` in components/ReferenceBoard.css; a column is 334 of them wide, the card of `FlowPart` too). A FlowPart's layer is now matched by its own box in `morph` (`glideSpan(…, coreOf)`), which fixes it for every section's cards on their own. Then the two steps (`besideOf`, `keysFor` in `morph`): when what is beside the cards (`.sec-after`) comes, the cards move over the first half of the move and it fades in over the second; when it goes, it fades out first and the cards move back after. Those two slides are twice as long so each step keeps the one speed (`TWO_STEPS` in the spec: `schedule/end`, `points/words`; "Reward Points…" is written as the missions move back). No recapture: the slides are the same.

About 0.8 s for the twelve. Their steps are named `phones n`, so that they are not the team's `mission n`.

The words of "Or To Teams…" and "Timer Colors…" are retyped on the board, so the slides the capture adds would go back to the spec's words and the pen would write them again; the phones have the video's 64.66 px on every slide. So, with `--video increased-efficiency-v3`: `text teams/end-5 bubble/0.0 "…"` to `teams/end-8`, `text urgency/end-4 bubble/0.0 "…"` and `urgency/end-5` (the words the slides before them have), `vmove individuals/end-3 board 64.66 0` and `end-4`, `move individuals/end-3 pointer-hint 64.66 0` and `end-4`. Then `vcomment-set increased-efficiency-v3 <video> <n> done=true`. Capture the whole board: `--only` writes a board of only those chapters. A dev server already running (the user's, on 3000) stops another `next dev` from starting: use it with `--site`, and leave it running.


## All storyboards: the videos scene by scene, editable, apart from the videos

The user, 2026-11-06: "for now hide all the other pages of storyboards, and make me a new one clean for all videos/storyboard, and put the increased efficiency video variation 4 in that storyboard by
just putting what's in the video scene by scene, make sure it's editable just like in figma ... the new storyboard will not be connected to videos at all, so if I change something there it shouldn't
change the video, but it should be connected to the video, meaning if I change a title position it and I hit apply to all insead of apply to unique, it should apply to the whole row of the same video,
I should be able to change UI text not just the titles, I should be able to delete items etc..". Written, NOT captured or tested by Claude.

- **The menu:** only All videos and All storyboards for now (`ONLY_BOARDS`, lib/storyboardAllStoryboards.mjs; set it to null to show every board again). The other boards are untouched and open by
  their address (`/storyboard?video=improved-quality-v2`...). `/storyboard` with no board opens All storyboards.
- **The board** (`all-storyboards`): public/storyboard/all-storyboards.json (its frames), content/storyboard/all-storyboards.json (what you edit). One ROW per video, one frame per scene: the title once
  its yellow line is drawn, then each sentence once its words are written and what it shows is done (its outlines still on). Its stage is `.ad-stage.rc-sb-stage` (the cuts' dark and font), and the
  storyboard loads the cuts' rules (components/remotion/CutParts.css, EfficiencyCut.css).
- **Making a row:** `node scripts/capture-cuts.mjs [--cut increased-efficiency-v3] [--row ie-v4] [--title "Increased Efficiency · Variation 4"]` with the site running. The cut (with the board's retyped
  words and its variation's layout) is drawn at each scene by /cut-stage (components/remotion/CutStage.jsx, Remotion's Thumbnail) in a headless Chrome and taken apart: every piece that names itself
  (`data-cut-part` in components/remotion/CutParts.jsx and EfficiencyCut.jsx) and is seen becomes a layer, its HTML as drawn, its box in stage px, its opacity as seen; a text of the band
  (`data-cut-pin`: the blue label, its line, the sentence) is put at its own box with its color written on it, so each one moves alone. **One click picks one thing** (the user, 2026-11-06: "I should
  be able to select one of them not the phone with it, I shouldn't have to double click to select ... each timer is selectable, avatars, row behind the timers"): a piece inside another piece (a
  phone's avatar: `Phone Adam F avatar`) or in a group that only holds pieces (`data-cut-group`: the closing shifts, each `Shift Adam F avatar`, `… row` with its hatched gaps, `… timer 1`…) is a
  layer of its own, put at its own box with the color and font it had there, and the piece it was in is drawn without it; a group is no layer (the shifts covered the whole picture). Name a new piece
  that way rather than leaving it inside another. Running it again makes that row's frames again; the other rows and what you edited (by frame id and piece key) stay.
- **Editing:** everything the board does (move, resize, scale, look, fill, retype any text, UI text too, delete, Shift+A pieces, the pencil...). A piece has the same key in every scene of its row, but
  nothing is linked (since 2026-11-09, "Nothing is linked" below): retyping its words, a move, a size, a look or a delete is that frame's
  alone until **Apply to all in this row** (the Asset section of the sidebar, and the right-click menu): what was done to it in this frame (moves, scales, sizes, styles, locks; taken out or
  not; never its words) is put on the same piece in every frame of the row (`applyAlongRow`); **Delete in the whole row** takes it out of all of them (`hideAlongRow`). Every board has these two now.
- **The background is fixed in place** (the user, 2026-11-06: "lock the background in all slides, to be editable in color but not position/scale"): a piece marked `data-cut-fixed` in the cut
  (`.rc-bg`, components/remotion/CutParts.jsx) is captured with `data-sb-fixed`, and the board (`isFixed` in components/storyboardDom.js) picks it and changes its fill and look, but has no handles for
  it, never drags, nudges, aligns, pastes a position on, resizes or turns it (its X / Y / W / H / rotation are greyed, a note says so), and a drag from it draws a rectangle; picked with others, the
  others move and it stays. It can still be deleted.
- **Not the video:** the board has no videos and feeds none; the films are made by their plans (lib/remotion/*.mjs), never from this board.

- **Its texts and its line (the user, 2026-11-06: "just make it as a sentence ... remove the black faded on top of the words", "resizing the rectangle should change line limit, but double clicking shouldn't, also make sure font size is editable from right side", "the line between text should be selectable and editable like any path"):** the band's dark fade is not captured. The title, each blue label and each sentence is ONE plain text in a TEXT BOX (`data-sb-box="text"`): its handles set its width (`sizeTo` keeps its height its words'), its lines wrap to it (`.sb-stage [data-sb-box="text"]` in Storyboard.css: white-space normal, height auto, so a retype never re-wraps it), its line height and letter spacing are in proportion to its size (the sidebar's font size keeps them). The line between the label and the sentence is a DRAWING like the pencil's (picked by its stroke; the sidebar's Drawing sets its color and width; on a captured drawing they are its style, `stroke` / `strokeWidth`, put on its path by `applyStyle`, so Undo and Apply to all in this row carry them; its handles set its length).
- **A mission is picked by its card (the user, 2026-11-09: "fix selection of missions to show selection only on mission"; written, NOT tested):** a mission of
  the cuts is laid out in a 1080 px board (its sizes are that board's units, `--ref-u`), so it cannot be trimmed like a phone; its piece says what is SEEN of it
  instead, `data-sb-seen=".chip"` (from `data-cut-seen` on the Card in components/remotion/EfficiencyCut.jsx, written by scripts/capture-cuts.mjs, which also
  records the card's box; the missions already captured were marked once, and a cut's card captured or pasted before is known by its class: `SEEN_BY_CLASS`).
  `seenOf` in components/storyboardDom.js: its outline, handles, snapping, rectangle picking and clicks are the card's; a click on the empty rest goes to what is
  under it; a pasted copy carries it. Use `data-cut-seen` for any other piece laid out bigger than it shows.
- **A piece is as big as what is seen of it (the user, 2026-11-06: "trim the selection to exactly the phone"):** a piece can name the element inside it that is its visible box (`data-cut-box`, a selector: the phones say `.ph-phone`, since a phone is laid out at its full 838 px and shrunk); the capture puts the piece at that box and moves what it holds back by as much, so the board's selection is exactly the phone (236 x 493), its face above it a piece of its own.
- **Its tablet is Variation 2's (the user, 2026-11-06: "change this tablet to the tablet we had in ... variation 2"):** the cuts' tablet (`.rc-device`, components/remotion/CutParts.css; the films too) is the tablet photo's outline (/tablet-frame.png) painted flat black, its screen `#F6F7F9`; the board's stage has no text color of its own (the white it had turned the tablet header's title and icons white on white). **The header has its own colors (the user, 2026-11-10: "fix UI in tablet the back, team board and + and burger menu, it looks gray it should be darker"; written, NOT tested):** the board's title has no color of its own and the storyboard page gives every button the text color round it (`.sb-root button { color:inherit }`), so on the dark page the title and the three round buttons were the page's light gray; now the title is black (`.rc-device .mi-department`) and the buttons' icons the product's blue `#1271b7` (`.ph-iconbtn` in `.rc-stage` and `.rc-sb-stage`: every cut, phones too, and the same pieces on All storyboards), in CutParts.css.

## Nothing is linked (the user, 2026-11-09)

"there is an issue with http://localhost:3000/storyboard, remove the link between slides, each slide is it's own slide, every text is not the same as
others, I should be able to add text, copy from somewhere else with no issue, nothing is linked. for example if I take a text from slide 5 that says 123
and I took it to slide 4, and slide 4 has text saying 234 ... if I change 123 to 000 in slide 4 it shouldn't change it in slide 5". Written, NOT tested.

- **Retyping a text changes that frame's text alone** (`retype` in components/Storyboard.jsx; the CLI's `text` too). Before, it also retyped the
  "duplicates" along the line that still said the same words (the same captured piece in the other frames of the row, pasted / Alt+dragged copies, the
  slides of a duplicated frame) until right-click > Make unique. Make unique and Link again are gone.
- **A copy is its own from the moment it is made** (lib/storyboardClipboard.mjs): pasted (Ctrl+V, on its own board or another), Alt+dragged, or in a
  duplicated frame (a snapshot of the frame's edits), it keeps the words, place and look it was copied with; retyping it, or what it came from, changes
  that one alone. A text "123" copied from slide 5 onto slide 4, where a text says "234", says 123 on slide 4; both of slide 4's texts and slide 5's are
  three different texts.
- **Only a button reaches the other frames:** **Apply to all in this row** (its place, size, scale, look, lock, a chat box's tail; never its words:
  `ROW_FIELDS` in lib/storyboardEdits.mjs) and **Delete in the whole row**; "the same piece" there is the captured asset with the same key in the other
  frames of the row (lib/storyboardLinks.mjs `linkOf`, `linkedLayers`); what was pasted, Alt+dragged, brought in with Shift+A or dropped is its frame's
  own. Copy / paste position on picked frames finds the asset by its key.
- **Older files:** an insert's `link` (what a copy was copied from) is dropped when the file is loaded and ignored if it is there; a frame's `unique`
  list is kept but means nothing.
- **Not changed: the videos.** A text retyped in a VIDEO (a double-click in a player) still changes the other slides of that video that show the very
  same chat box (`info.sameWords`), one caption seen across a scene's clicks, so the words do not jump while it plays. The video text editor's Make
  unique / Apply to all (its look) is another thing and stays.
